BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
import os,glob,json,subprocess,hashlib,collections
from pathlib import Path
W=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv'); R=Path.cwd()
roots=[str(Path.home()/'.codex/sessions'),str(Path.home()/'.codex/archived_sessions'),str(Path.home()/'.codex/history.jsonl'),str(Path.home()/'.bash_history'),'/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/HarnessGrad-evidence-archive','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/hg-backup-20261014','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/hg-backup-20261014-pre-rewrite','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/hg-logs','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/harnessgrad_work','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/harnessgrad_work','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/harnessgrad_cli','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/HarnessGrad-real-work-20261117','/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/HarnessGrad-real-work-20261117c',str(R/'runs'),str(R/'runs_discarded_20260929-004559'),str(R/'.harnessgrad-state'),str(R/'tests/fixtures'),'/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-census']
pattern=r'fever|copenlu|create_nli_dataset|aggregate_papelo_nei|merge_nli_files|ae61c5b6cb11eafcc7d6c33069c68dd5c848f91f|5da0ccc0ccf77f974611de13f8aac6f78c6bba6293912835099eb6029baa85d9'
summary=[]; matches=[]; errors=[]
with (W/'scope-files.jsonl').open('w') as inv:
 for n,root in enumerate(roots):
  paths=[]; errs=[]
  if os.path.isfile(root): paths=[root]
  elif os.path.isdir(root):
   for a,ds,fs in os.walk(root,onerror=lambda e:errs.append(str(e))):
    ds.sort()
    for f in sorted(fs):
     p=os.path.join(a,f)
     if os.path.isfile(p): paths.append(p)
  total=0
  for p in paths:
   try:
    size=os.path.getsize(p); total+=size; inv.write(json.dumps({'scope':n,'path':p,'bytes':size})+'\n')
   except OSError as e: errs.append(str(e))
  out=W/f'rg-{n:02d}.txt'; err=W/f'rg-{n:02d}.stderr'
  if os.path.exists(root):
   cmd=['rg','-l','-i','--hidden','--no-ignore','--glob','!.git/**',pattern,root]
   with out.open('w') as o,err.open('w') as e: result=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=o,stderr=e)
   hits=out.read_text().splitlines(); code=result.returncode
  else: hits=[]; code=None; out.write_text('');err.write_text('')
  summary.append({'scope':n,'root':root,'exists':os.path.exists(root),'files':len(paths),'bytes':total,'rg_exit':code,'hit_files':len(hits),'walk_errors':errs,'rg_stderr':err.read_text()})
  matches+=hits
(W/'scope-summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n')
(W/'candidate-files.txt').write_text('\n'.join(dict.fromkeys(matches))+'\n')
print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
print('unique candidate files',len(set(matches)))
END EXACT PROGRAM

PROGRAM /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/git-search.py
BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
import subprocess,re,json
from pathlib import Path
W=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv')
commands={'git-log.txt':['git','log','--all','--format=fuller','--name-only'],'git-stash.txt':['git','stash','list'],'git-reflog.txt':['git','reflog','--all','--format=%H %gd %gs']}
for token in ['fever_gold_evidence','create_nli_dataset','merge_nli_files','aggregate_papelo_nei','original_id','valid.jsonl']:
 commands['git-S-'+token+'.txt']=['git','log','--all','--format=%H %s','-S'+token,'--']
for name,cmd in commands.items():
 r=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE);(W/name).write_bytes(r.stdout)
 print(json.dumps({'command':cmd,'exit':r.returncode,'lines':len(r.stdout.splitlines()),'stderr':r.stderr.decode()}))
rows=[l.split() for l in (W/'all-objects.txt').read_text().splitlines()]
p=subprocess.Popen(['git','cat-file','--batch'],stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
pat=re.compile(rb'fever|copenlu|create_nli_dataset|aggregate_papelo_nei|merge_nli_files',re.I)
hits=[]
with (W/'object-hits.jsonl').open('w') as out:
 for h,t,n in rows:
  if t not in ('blob','commit'): continue
  p.stdin.write(h.encode()+b'\n');p.stdin.flush();header=p.stdout.readline();body=p.stdout.read(int(n));assert p.stdout.read(1)==b'\n'
  if pat.search(body):
   hit={'object':h,'type':t,'bytes':int(n),'matches':[{'line':i,'text':l.decode('utf-8',errors='replace')[:2500]} for i,l in enumerate(body.splitlines(),1) if pat.search(l)]}
   out.write(json.dumps(hit,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n'); hits.append((h,t))
p.stdin.close();p.wait()
print(json.dumps({'objects_enumerated':len(rows),'blobs_commits_searched':sum(t in ('blob','commit') for h,t,n in rows),'hit_objects':len(hits),'hit_types':{t:sum(x[1]==t for x in hits) for t in ('blob','commit')}}))
END EXACT PROGRAM

PROGRAM /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/inspect-sessions.py
BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
import json,re,collections
from pathlib import Path
W=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv');out=(W/'session-hits.jsonl').open('w');cnt=collections.Counter(); files=collections.Counter()
pat=re.compile(r'fever|copenlu|create_nli_dataset|aggregate_papelo_nei|merge_nli_files',re.I)
for f in (W/'candidate-files.txt').read_text().splitlines():
 if '/.codex/' not in f: continue
 for n,line in enumerate(open(f),1):
  if not pat.search(line): continue
  try: obj=json.loads(line)
  except ValueError: continue
  pay=obj.get('payload',{}); typ=pay.get('type',obj.get('type'))
  body=pay.get('arguments') or pay.get('output') or pay.get('content') or pay.get('message') or pay.get('text') or pay
  txt=body if isinstance(body,str) else json.dumps(body,ensure_ascii=False)
  matches=list(pat.finditer(txt))
  if not matches: continue
  snippets=[txt[max(0,m.start()-180):min(len(txt),m.end()+400)] for m in matches]
  hit={'file':f,'line':n,'timestamp':obj.get('timestamp'),'type':typ,'name':pay.get('name'),'call_id':pay.get('call_id'),'role':pay.get('role'),'characters':len(txt),'snippets':snippets}
  out.write(json.dumps(hit,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n');cnt[typ]+=1;files[f]+=1
out.close()
print('types',dict(cnt),'files',len(files))
print('files ordered by name')
for f in sorted(files):
 if '/2026/10/0' in f: print(files[f],f)
END EXACT PROGRAM

PROGRAM /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/backup-search.py
BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,json,re,zipfile
W=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv');pat=re.compile(rb'fever|copenlu|create_nli_dataset|aggregate_papelo_nei|merge_nli_files',re.I)
for tag in ['hg-backup-20261014','hg-backup-20261014-pre-rewrite']:
 gd='/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/'+tag+'/dot-git';cmd=['git','--git-dir='+gd,'cat-file','--batch-all-objects','--batch-check'];r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,check=True);(W/(tag+'-objects.txt')).write_bytes(r.stdout)
 p=subprocess.Popen(['git','--git-dir='+gd,'cat-file','--batch'],stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
 counts={};hits=[]
 for line in r.stdout.splitlines():
  h,t,n=line.decode().split();counts[t]=counts.get(t,0)+1
  if t not in ('blob','commit'):continue
  p.stdin.write(h.encode()+b'\n');p.stdin.flush();p.stdout.readline();body=p.stdout.read(int(n));assert p.stdout.read(1)==b'\n'
  if pat.search(body): hits.append({'object':h,'type':t,'bytes':int(n),'matches':[{'line':i,'text':l.decode(errors='replace')[:3000]} for i,l in enumerate(body.splitlines(),1) if pat.search(l)]})
 p.stdin.close();p.wait();(W/(tag+'-hits.json')).write_text(json.dumps(hits,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
 print(json.dumps({'git_dir':gd,'object_counts':counts,'hit_objects':len(hits)}))
for x in map(json.loads,(W/'scope-files.jsonl').open()):
 if x['scope']!=4 or not x['path'].endswith('.zip'): continue
 with zipfile.ZipFile(x['path']) as z:
  hits=[i.filename for i in z.infolist() if not i.is_dir() and pat.search(z.read(i))]
  print(json.dumps({'zip':x['path'],'members':len(z.infolist()),'hit_members':hits}))
END EXACT PROGRAM

PROGRAM /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/embedded-search.py
BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
from pathlib import Path
import json,subprocess,re,tarfile
W=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv');pat=re.compile(rb'fever|copenlu|create_nli_dataset|aggregate_papelo_nei|merge_nli_files',re.I)
roots=set()
for x in map(json.loads,(W/'scope-files.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()+(W/'main-readonly-search/scope-files.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()):
 if '/.git/' in x['path']: roots.add(x['path'].split('/.git/')[0]+'/.git')
results=[]
for gd in sorted(roots):
 r=subprocess.run(['git','--git-dir='+gd,'cat-file','--batch-all-objects','--batch-check'],capture_output=True);rows=r.stdout.splitlines(); hits=[]; counts={}
 if r.returncode:results.append({'git_dir':gd,'exit':r.returncode,'stderr':r.stderr.decode()});continue
 p=subprocess.Popen(['git','--git-dir='+gd,'cat-file','--batch'],stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
 for line in rows:
  h,t,n=line.decode().split();counts[t]=counts.get(t,0)+1
  if t not in ('blob','commit'):continue
  p.stdin.write(h.encode()+b'\n');p.stdin.flush();p.stdout.readline();body=p.stdout.read(int(n));assert p.stdout.read(1)==b'\n'
  if pat.search(body):hits.append({'object':h,'type':t})
 p.stdin.close();p.wait();results.append({'git_dir':gd,'exit':r.returncode,'object_counts':counts,'hit_objects':hits})
(W/'embedded-git-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({'embedded_git_dirs':len(roots),'errors':sum(x['exit']!=0 for x in results),'objects':sum(sum(x.get('object_counts',{}).values()) for x in results),'hit_objects':sum(len(x.get('hit_objects',[])) for x in results)}))
for x in map(json.loads,(W/'main-readonly-search/scope-files.jsonl').open()):
 if x['path'].endswith('.tar.gz'):
  with tarfile.open(x['path'],'r:gz') as t:
   members=t.getmembers();hits=[m.name for m in members if m.isfile() and pat.search(t.extractfile(m).read())]
   print(json.dumps({'tar':x['path'],'members':len(members),'hit_members':hits}))
END EXACT PROGRAM

PROGRAM /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/parse-installed.py
BEGIN EXACT PROGRAM
from pathlib import Path
import json,hashlib,collections,unicodedata
p=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/datasets/fever/valid.jsonl');raw=p.read_bytes();rows=[json.loads(l) for l in raw.splitlines()];d=collections.Counter();sizes=collections.Counter();exposed=[];examples={}
for i,r in enumerate(rows):
 pairs=[(unicodedata.normalize('NFC',e[0]),int(e[1])) for e in r['evidence']];n=len(set(pairs));nei=r['label']=='NOT ENOUGH INFO'
 d['rows']+=1;d['NEI' if nei else 'non_NEI']+=1
 if not nei:
  sizes[n]+=1
  if n>1:
   exposed.append({'task_index':i,'installed_line':i+1,'id':r['id'],'original_id':r['original_id'],'pairs':pairs})
   if str(n) not in examples:examples[str(n)]={'task_index':i,'installed_line':i+1,'original_id':r['original_id'],'pairs':pairs}
  d['non_NEI_pair_count_ne_distinct']+=len(pairs)!=n
  d['non_NEI_missing_pair_fields']+=any(e[0] is None or e[1] is None for e in r['evidence'])
  d['non_NEI_line_fields_not_strings']+=any(not isinstance(e[1],str) for e in r['evidence'])
(W:=Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv')).joinpath('exposed-rows.json').write_text(json.dumps(exposed,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({'path':str(p),'bytes':len(raw),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),'counts':dict(d),'non_NEI_distinct_pair_size_distribution':dict(sorted(sizes.items())),'examples_by_group_size':examples},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
END EXACT PROGRAM

"/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-wt/w-fevconv\n"
"# HarnessGrad Agent Operating Rules\n"
"\n"
"## Execution priorities\n"
"\n"
"1. Close automatically solvable blockers and internalize reusable platform capabilities before launching large pilots.\n"
"2. Use isolated Codex worktrees only. Parent reviews every diff, runs focused tests, rejects stale reports and unrelated changes, then merges safe commits and updates the journal.\n"
"3. Keep up to 10 independent Codex tasks active when work can be parallelized. Every task must have a unique output filename; never trust inherited `reports/report.md` without checking the diff and commit.\n"
"4. Avoid idle rounds: combine waits with concrete receipt inspection, diff review, test execution, journal work or launch preparation. Do not spend a round only polling unchanged status.\n"
"\n"
"## Working copies\n"
"\n"
"There are exactly **two writers**, and the rule is about who yields to whom:\n"
"\n"
"- **The operator writes directly to the main checkout** `/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/Study/HarnessGrad`\n"
"  whenever they want. That is supported, not a conflict. An agent merges **on top of** whatever\n"
"  it finds there and never rewrites, rebases away, reverts or moves an operator commit. When a\n"
"  merge conflicts because the operator changed the same file, the operator's change is the\n"
"  starting point and the agent's is the one that has to fit around it.\n"
"- **Agents never commit to `main`.** They work in registered `git worktree` checkouts under\n"
"  `/tmp/hg-*`, commit there, and the parent reviews the diff and merges.\n"
"- Every *other* copy of this repository on this machine is read-only or disposable.\n"
"- **Never create a full clone of this repository inside `Study/`.** Two were made on 2026-11-17\n"
"  (`HarnessGrad-appworld-controls-20261117`, `HarnessGrad-gaia-controls-20261117`). Both are now\n"
"  stranded on pre-rewrite commits that do not exist in `main`'s object store, and both still\n"
"  carry the old layout (`web/dist`, root-level report files). Their `origin` remotes -- which\n"
"  pointed at the main checkout, fetch **and push** -- have been removed, because a single\n"
"  `git push --force` from either would have restored the old history over `main`.\n"
"- They are kept, not deleted, and not reset: they hold run receipts and evidence\n"
"  (`gaia-evidence/`, `gaia-evidence-all17/`, `GAIA_FINAL_LAUNCH_RECEIPT.json`, the appworld\n"
"  `deliverables/` and `runtime/`) that exist nowhere else, and rule 5 of the staged pilot policy\n"
"  says old and incomplete receipts are preserved. Treat them as frozen evidence: read them, never\n"
"  fetch, merge, push or \"update\" them.\n"
"- 10 of 20 local branches also still point at pre-rewrite commits. They are refs, not working\n"
"  copies, so they are not a writing hazard, but they keep the old history reachable; decide\n"
"  explicitly before deleting any, since they are the only record of that codex work.\n"
"- If a copy is ever needed for an isolated run, make it a `git worktree` (registered, so\n"
"  `git worktree list` shows it) rather than a clone, and remove it when the run is done.\n"
"\n"
"## Test execution\n"
"\n"
"Pytest `--basetemp` must be **OUTSIDE the platform tree**, for example\n"
"`--basetemp=/tmp/harnessgrad-pytest-quality`. Driver-based tests use `tmp_path`\n"
"as a harness work root, which the runner correctly refuses inside the platform.\n"
"Omitting the option preserves pytest's normal default.\n"
"\n"
"## GPU execution\n"
"\n"
"- **One local endpoint: `http://127.0.0.1:8002/v1`** (model `qwen3.5-9b-local`). GPU0 and the\n"
"  `8001` server were released on 2026-10-14 to give a card back; do not start a second one.\n"
"- `eval/unix_model_relay.py` hard-codes the agent model route to `127.0.0.1:8002`, so `8002` is\n"
"  the endpoint a containerised harness actually reaches. That, not preference, is why this is\n"
"  the one that was kept -- the other would have needed a production change.\n"
"- Keep the endpoint productively occupied with necessary no-model controls, diagnostics or valid\n"
"  pilots; never create duplicate, fake or scientifically unjustified work for utilization. If\n"
"  there is no approved diagnostic or pilot, an idle loaded server is the correct state: record\n"
"  that rather than inventing work.\n"
"- Bind each run to one endpoint and one fresh run ID. Keep total H0 concurrency `<=3`, `jobs=1`.\n"
"\n"
"## Staged pilot policy\n"
"\n"
"- First run a one-task/one-trial diagnostic after all dataset gates pass.\n"
"- For temporary reruns of blocked/incomplete datasets, select `min(30, ceil(10% * eligible))` tasks before execution, with `trials=1`, `jobs=1`.\n"
"- Expand only after a separate review of a valid measured diagnostic receipt. Do not silently revert to the historical `min(50, ceil(10% * eligible)) x 3` protocol.\n"
"- Invalid, unmeasured, timeout, scorer, provider, cleanup or snapshot failures are not ordinary zero scores.\n"
"- Preserve old and incomplete receipts; never resume or overwrite an old run ID.\n"
"\n"
"## Dataset taxonomy\n"
"\n"
"Task capability is a cross-table: `demand x surface`. Harness coverage is a separate axis: `exercises`. Do not collapse these into one flat dataset category.\n"
"\n"
"## Prohibited shortcuts\n"
"\n"
"No method tuning, paid endpoints, heldout leakage, SOTUQA, API-Bank, NESTFUL, Tau2-bench, Matrix, score fabrication or fake benchmark utilization.\n"
"\n"
"## Evidence requirements\n"
"\n"
"Every diagnostic or pilot records metric identity, eligible/selected/task/trial coverage, invalid and unmeasured counts, source/split/manifest/model/harness hashes, endpoint binding, resource/budget limits, cleanup receipt and comparability notes.\n"
"\n"
"## Blocker closure priorities\n"
"\n"
"Prioritize selected-only/privacy and provenance, scorer dependency/network and offline closure, trusted verifier/runtime, artifact-only state export, filesystem quota enforcement, official scorer execution, snapshot lifecycle and cleanup. A passing synthetic control is not a production H0 authorization.\n"

"     1\tr\"\"\"Transcribed official evidence metrics used by FEVER and HotpotQA adapters.\n"
"     2\t\n"
"     3\tThe optional `prediction.json` contract carries those evidence fields. Missing evidence\n"
"     4\tis represented by `MissingEvidence`; each adapter maps that valid-but-incomplete\n"
"     5\tprediction to an official measured zero. Malformed artifact structure is rejected by\n"
"     6\t`data/scaffolds/qa/prediction.py` and is unmeasured by the verifier.\n"
"     7\t\n"
"     8\tThe adapters import this standard-library-only module into their isolated verifier.\n"
"     9\t\"\"\"\n"
"    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n"
"    11\t\n"
"    12\tfrom collections import Counter\n"
"    13\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n"
"    14\tfrom typing import Any, Sequence\n"
"    15\t\n"
"    16\timport re\n"
"    17\timport string\n"
"    18\t\n"
"    19\t\n"
"    20\tclass MissingEvidence(Exception):\n"
"    21\t    \"\"\"The input has no evidence field, so this metric **cannot be computed**.\n"
"    22\t\n"
"    23\t    Distinct from \"the score is 0\". A nearby number reported in place of it makes an\n"
"    24\t    interface gap and a wrong answer look the same in a table -- the error\n"
"    25\t    `docs/dataset-onboarding-skill.md` §1 is about.\n"
"    26\t    \"\"\"\n"
"    27\t\n"
"    28\t\n"
"    29\t# --------------------------------------------------------------- HotpotQA ---\n"
"    30\t# `hotpotqa/hotpot/hotpot_evaluate_v1.py`, transcribed.\n"
"    31\t\n"
"    32\t\n"
"    33\tdef hotpot_normalize_answer(s: str) -> str:\n"
"    34\t    \"\"\"`hotpot_evaluate_v1.py::normalize_answer`, verbatim.\"\"\"\n"
"    35\t\n"
"    36\t    def remove_articles(text: str) -> str:\n"
"    37\t        return re.sub(r\"\\b(a|an|the)\\b\", \" \", text)\n"
"    38\t\n"
"    39\t    def white_space_fix(text: str) -> str:\n"
"    40\t        return \" \".join(text.split())\n"
"    41\t\n"
"    42\t    def remove_punc(text: str) -> str:\n"
"    43\t        return \"\".join(ch for ch in text if ch not in set(string.punctuation))\n"
"    44\t\n"
"    45\t    def lower(text: str) -> str:\n"
"    46\t        return text.lower()\n"
"    47\t\n"
"    48\t    return white_space_fix(remove_articles(remove_punc(lower(str(s)))))\n"
"    49\t\n"
"    50\t\n"
"    51\tdef hotpot_f1_score(prediction: str, ground_truth: str) -> tuple[float, float, float]:\n"
"    52\t    \"\"\"`hotpot_evaluate_v1.py::f1_score`, verbatim. Returns `(f1, precision, recall)`.\"\"\"\n"
"    53\t    p, g = hotpot_normalize_answer(prediction), hotpot_normalize_answer(ground_truth)\n"
"    54\t    if p in (\"yes\", \"no\", \"noanswer\") and p != g:\n"
"    55\t        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n"
"    56\t    if g in (\"yes\", \"no\", \"noanswer\") and p != g:\n"
"    57\t        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n"
"    58\t    pt, gt = p.split(), g.split()\n"
"    59\t    common = Counter(pt) & Counter(gt)\n"
"    60\t    num_same = sum(common.values())\n"
"    61\t    if num_same == 0:\n"
"    62\t        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n"
"    63\t    precision = 1.0 * num_same / len(pt)\n"
"    64\t    recall = 1.0 * num_same / len(gt)\n"
"    65\t    return (2 * precision * recall) / (precision + recall), precision, recall\n"
"    66\t\n"
"    67\t\n"
"    68\tdef hotpot_exact_match(prediction: str, ground_truth: str) -> float:\n"
"    69\t    \"\"\"`hotpot_evaluate_v1.py::exact_match_score`, verbatim.\"\"\"\n"
"    70\t    return float(hotpot_normalize_answer(prediction) == hotpot_normalize_answer(ground_truth))\n"
"    71\t\n"
"    72\t\n"
"    73\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n"
"    74\tclass SpScore:\n"
"    75\t    \"\"\"Supporting-facts scores, named rather than positional.\n"
"    76\t\n"
"    77\t    Upstream `update_sp` returns `(em, prec, recall)` -- three floats, and `f1` is only\n"
"    78\t    accumulated into the metrics dict. A first version of this transcription returned\n"
"    79\t    `(em, f1, prec)`; a caller unpacking `sp_em, sp_prec, sp_recall` would then have used\n"
"    80\t    f1 as precision, and every number on the page would have looked normal. An interface\n"
"    81\t    whose order can be wrong should not be expressed as an order.\n"
"    82\t    \"\"\"\n"
"    83\t\n"
"    84\t    em: float\n"
"    85\t    f1: float\n"
"    86\t    prec: float\n"
"    87\t    recall: float\n"
"    88\t\n"
"    89\t\n"
"    90\tdef hotpot_update_sp(sp_prediction: Sequence[Sequence[Any]] | None,\n"
"    91\t                     sp_gold: Sequence[Sequence[Any]] | None) -> SpScore:\n"
"    92\t    \"\"\"`hotpot_evaluate_v1.py::update_sp`, with the missing-field guard.\n"
"    93\t\n"
"    94\t    `em = 1.0 if fp + fn == 0 else 0.0` -- exact set equality, not F1.\n"
"    95\t    \"\"\"\n"
"    96\t    if not sp_prediction:\n"
"    97\t        raise MissingEvidence(\n"
"    98\t            \"HotpotQA supporting-fact evidence is absent; the contract treats this \"\n"
"    99\t            \"as an incorrect prediction when scoring, rather than an invalid artifact.\")\n"
"   100\t    cur = set(map(tuple, sp_prediction))\n"
"   101\t    gold = set(map(tuple, sp_gold or []))\n"
"   102\t    tp = sum(1 for e in cur if e in gold)\n"
"   103\t    fp = sum(1 for e in cur if e not in gold)\n"
"   104\t    fn = sum(1 for e in gold if e not in cur)\n"
"   105\t    prec = tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp > 0 else 0.0\n"
"   106\t    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn > 0 else 0.0\n"
"   107\t    f1 = 2 * prec * recall / (prec + recall) if prec + recall > 0 else 0.0\n"
"   108\t    return SpScore(em=1.0 if fp + fn == 0 else 0.0, f1=f1, prec=prec, recall=recall)\n"
"   109\t\n"
"   110\t\n"
"   111\tdef hotpot_joint(em: float, prec: float, recall: float, sp: SpScore) -> tuple[float, float]:\n"
"   112\t    \"\"\"`hotpot_evaluate_v1.py::eval`'s joint lines, verbatim.\n"
"   113\t\n"
"   114\t    `joint_em = em * sp_em`; `joint_prec = prec * sp_prec`, `joint_recall = recall *\n"
"   115\t    sp_recall`, and `joint_f1` is the harmonic mean of the two joint components.\n"
"   116\t    \"\"\"\n"
"   117\t    joint_prec = prec * sp.prec\n"
"   118\t    joint_recall = recall * sp.recall\n"
"   119\t    joint_f1 = (2 * joint_prec * joint_recall / (joint_prec + joint_recall)\n"
"   120\t                if joint_prec + joint_recall > 0 else 0.0)\n"
"   121\t    return em * sp.em, joint_f1\n"
"   122\t\n"
"   123\t\n"
"   124\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------- FEVER ---\n"
"   125\t# `sheffieldnlp/fever-scorer/src/fever/scorer.py`, transcribed.\n"
"   126\t\n"
"   127\t\n"
"   128\tdef fever_is_correct_label(label: str, predicted_label: str) -> bool:\n"
"   129\t    \"\"\"`scorer.py::is_correct_label`, verbatim.\n"
"   130\t\n"
"   131\t    `.upper()` and nothing else -- **no punctuation stripping**. Measured consequence,\n"
"   132\t    pinned by the tests: `NOT ENOUGH INFO.` (one trailing full stop) is wrong. That is\n"
"   133\t    upstream's strictness, not an implementation shortcut, and \"fixing\" it here would\n"
"   134\t    produce a number higher than the published one.\n"
"   135\t    \"\"\"\n"
"   136\t    return str(label).upper() == str(predicted_label).upper()\n"
"   137\t\n"
"   138\t\n"
"   139\tdef fever_is_strictly_correct(label: str, predicted_label: str,\n"
"   140\t                              evidence: Sequence[Any] | None,\n"
"   141\t                              predicted_evidence: Sequence[Any] | None,\n"
"   142\t                              *, max_evidence: int = 5) -> bool:\n"
"   143\t    \"\"\"`scorer.py::is_strictly_correct`, with the missing-field guard.\n"
"   144\t\n"
"   145\t    A label is strictly correct when it is right **and** one whole gold evidence group is\n"
"   146\t    contained in the first `max_evidence` predictions. `NOT ENOUGH INFO` items are exempt\n"
"   147\t    from evidence -- that branch returns immediately, which is why an \"answer-only FEVER\n"
"   148\t    score\" is not even the same rule for the three classes.\n"
"   149\t    \"\"\"\n"
"   150\t    if str(label).upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and not predicted_evidence:\n"
"   151\t        raise MissingEvidence(\n"
"   152\t            \"FEVER predicted_evidence is absent; the contract treats this as an \"\n"
"   153\t            \"incorrect prediction when scoring, rather than an invalid artifact.\")\n"
"   154\t    correct = fever_is_correct_label(label, predicted_label)\n"
"   155\t    if str(label).upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and correct:\n"
"   156\t        head = [[e[0], e[1]] for e in (predicted_evidence or [])[:max_evidence]]\n"
"   157\t        groups = evidence or []\n"
"   158\t        if groups and groups[0] and isinstance(groups[0][0], str):\n"
"   159\t            # The pinned Hub mirror flattens the upstream group id: each available\n"
"   160\t            # sentence is retained as an independently sufficient evidence group.\n"
"   161\t            groups = [[item] for item in groups]\n"
"   162\t        for group in groups:\n"
"   163\t            actual = [[e[0], int(e[1])] if len(e) == 3 else [e[2], e[3]]\n"
"   164\t                      for e in group]\n"
"   165\t            if all(sentence in head for sentence in actual):\n"
"   166\t                return True\n"
"   167\t        return False\n"
"   168\t    if str(label).upper() == \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and correct:\n"
"   169\t        return True\n"
"   170\t    return False\n"
"   171\t\n"
"   172\t\n"
"   173\t__all__ = [\"MissingEvidence\", \"SpScore\",\n"
"   174\t           \"hotpot_normalize_answer\", \"hotpot_f1_score\", \"hotpot_exact_match\",\n"
"   175\t           \"hotpot_update_sp\", \"hotpot_joint\",\n"
"   176\t           \"fever_is_correct_label\", \"fever_is_strictly_correct\"]\n"

"import six\n"
"\n"
"def check_predicted_evidence_format(instance):\n"
"    if 'predicted_evidence' in instance.keys() and len(instance['predicted_evidence']):\n"
"        assert all(isinstance(prediction, list)\n"
"                   for prediction in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"]), \\\n"
"            \"Predicted evidence must be a list of (page,line) lists\"\n"
"\n"
"        assert all(len(prediction) == 2\n"
"                   for prediction in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"]), \\\n"
"            \"Predicted evidence must be a list of (page,line) lists\"\n"
"\n"
"        assert all(isinstance(prediction[0], six.string_types)\n"
"                    for prediction in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"]), \\\n"
"            \"Predicted evidence must be a list of (page<string>,line<int>) lists\"\n"
"\n"
"        assert all(isinstance(prediction[1], int)\n"
"                   for prediction in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"]), \\\n"
"            \"Predicted evidence must be a list of (page<string>,line<int>) lists\"\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def is_correct_label(instance):\n"
"    return instance[\"label\"].upper() == instance[\"predicted_label\"].upper()\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def is_strictly_correct(instance, max_evidence=None):\n"
"    #Strict evidence matching is only for NEI class\n"
"    check_predicted_evidence_format(instance)\n"
"\n"
"    if instance[\"label\"].upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and is_correct_label(instance):\n"
"        assert 'predicted_evidence' in instance, \"Predicted evidence must be provided for strict scoring\"\n"
"\n"
"        if max_evidence is None:\n"
"            max_evidence = len(instance[\"predicted_evidence\"])\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"        for evience_group in instance[\"evidence\"]:\n"
"            #Filter out the annotation ids. We just want the evidence page and line number\n"
"            actual_sentences = [[e[2], e[3]] for e in evience_group]\n"
"            #Only return true if an entire group of actual sentences is in the predicted sentences\n"
"            if all([actual_sent in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"][:max_evidence] for actual_sent in actual_sentences]):\n"
"                return True\n"
"\n"
"    #If the class is NEI, we don't score the evidence retrieval component\n"
"    elif instance[\"label\"].upper() == \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and is_correct_label(instance):\n"
"        return True\n"
"\n"
"    return False\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def evidence_macro_precision(instance, max_evidence=None):\n"
"    this_precision = 0.0\n"
"    this_precision_hits = 0.0\n"
"\n"
"    if instance[\"label\"].upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\":\n"
"        all_evi = [[e[2], e[3]] for eg in instance[\"evidence\"] for e in eg if e[3] is not None]\n"
"\n"
"        predicted_evidence = instance[\"predicted_evidence\"] if max_evidence is None else \\\n"
"                                                                        instance[\"predicted_evidence\"][:max_evidence]\n"
"\n"
"        for prediction in predicted_evidence:\n"
"            if prediction in all_evi:\n"
"                this_precision += 1.0\n"
"            this_precision_hits += 1.0\n"
"\n"
"        return (this_precision / this_precision_hits) if this_precision_hits > 0 else 1.0, 1.0\n"
"\n"
"    return 0.0, 0.0\n"
"\n"
"def evidence_macro_recall(instance, max_evidence=None):\n"
"    # We only want to score F1/Precision/Recall of recalled evidence for NEI claims\n"
"    if instance[\"label\"].upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\":\n"
"        # If there's no evidence to predict, return 1\n"
"        if len(instance[\"evidence\"]) == 0 or all([len(eg) == 0 for eg in instance]):\n"
"           return 1.0, 1.0\n"
"\n"
"        predicted_evidence = instance[\"predicted_evidence\"] if max_evidence is None else \\\n"
"                                                                        instance[\"predicted_evidence\"][:max_evidence]\n"
"\n"
"        for evidence_group in instance[\"evidence\"]:\n"
"            evidence = [[e[2], e[3]] for e in evidence_group]\n"
"            if all([item in predicted_evidence for item in evidence]):\n"
"                # We only want to score complete groups of evidence. Incomplete groups are worthless.\n"
"                return 1.0, 1.0\n"
"        return 0.0, 1.0\n"
"    return 0.0, 0.0\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"# Micro is not used. This code is just included to demostrate our model of macro/micro\n"
"def evidence_micro_precision(instance):\n"
"    this_precision = 0\n"
"    this_precision_hits = 0\n"
"\n"
"    # We only want to score Macro F1/Precision/Recall of recalled evidence for NEI claims\n"
"    if instance[\"label\"].upper() != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\":\n"
"        all_evi = [[e[2], e[3]] for eg in instance[\"evidence\"] for e in eg if e[3] is not None]\n"
"\n"
"        for prediction in instance[\"predicted_evidence\"]:\n"
"            if prediction in all_evi:\n"
"                this_precision += 1.0\n"
"            this_precision_hits += 1.0\n"
"\n"
"    return this_precision, this_precision_hits\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def fever_score(predictions,actual=None, max_evidence=5):\n"
"    correct = 0\n"
"    strict = 0\n"
"\n"
"    macro_precision = 0\n"
"    macro_precision_hits = 0\n"
"\n"
"    macro_recall = 0\n"
"    macro_recall_hits = 0\n"
"\n"
"    for idx,instance in enumerate(predictions):\n"
"        assert 'predicted_evidence' in instance.keys(), 'evidence must be provided for the prediction'\n"
"\n"
"        #If it's a blind test set, we need to copy in the values from the actual data\n"
"        if 'evidence' not in instance or 'label' not in instance:\n"
"            assert actual is not None, 'in blind evaluation mode, actual data must be provided'\n"
"            assert len(actual) == len(predictions), 'actual data and predicted data length must match'\n"
"            assert 'evidence' in actual[idx].keys(), 'evidence must be provided for the actual evidence'\n"
"            instance['evidence'] = actual[idx]['evidence']\n"
"            instance['label'] = actual[idx]['label']\n"
"\n"
"        assert 'evidence' in instance.keys(), 'gold evidence must be provided'\n"
"\n"
"        if is_correct_label(instance):\n"
"            correct += 1.0\n"
"\n"
"            if is_strictly_correct(instance, max_evidence):\n"
"                strict+=1.0\n"
"\n"
"        macro_prec = evidence_macro_precision(instance, max_evidence)\n"
"        macro_precision += macro_prec[0]\n"
"        macro_precision_hits += macro_prec[1]\n"
"\n"
"        macro_rec = evidence_macro_recall(instance, max_evidence)\n"
"        macro_recall += macro_rec[0]\n"
"        macro_recall_hits += macro_rec[1]\n"
"\n"
"    total = len(predictions)\n"
"\n"
"    strict_score = strict / total\n"
"    acc_score = correct / total\n"
"\n"
"    pr = (macro_precision / macro_precision_hits) if macro_precision_hits > 0 else 1.0\n"
"    rec = (macro_recall / macro_recall_hits) if macro_recall_hits > 0 else 0.0\n"
"\n"
"    f1 = 2.0 * pr * rec / (pr + rec)\n"
"\n"
"    return strict_score, acc_score, pr, rec, f1\"\"\"\n"
"Create FEVER NLI dataset, where for each line in the original FEVER dataset\n"
"with N number of evidence annotations, we create N instances with the\n"
"original fields and evidence - list of sentences # [Wikipedia URL, sentence ID, sentence]\n"
"from the wiki dump.\n"
"\n"
"'NOT ENOUGH INFO' instances are discarded as they do not have any evidence annotations.\n"
"\"\"\"\n"
"import argparse\n"
"import json\n"
"import os\n"
"import unicodedata\n"
"from tqdm import tqdm\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n"
"    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n"
"    parser.add_argument(\"--wiki_dir\",\n"
"                        help=\"Path to the wiki pages dump\",\n"
"                        required=True, type=str)\n"
"    parser.add_argument(\"--dataset_dirs\",\n"
"                        help=\"Paths to the dataset splits\",\n"
"                        required=True, type=str, nargs='+')\n"
"    parser.add_argument(\"--output_paths\",\n"
"                        help=\"Paths to serialize the datasets\",\n"
"                        required=True, type=str, nargs='+')\n"
"    args = parser.parse_args()\n"
"\n"
"    wiki_docs = {}  # wiki_id: [wiki lines]\n"
"\n"
"    for file in tqdm(os.scandir(args.wiki_dir), desc='Reading wiki pages...', leave=True):\n"
"        # {\"id\": \"\", \"text\": \"\", \"lines\": \"\"}\n"
"        # \"lines\": \"0\\tThe following are the football -LRB-\n"
"        # soccer -RRB- events of the year 1928 throughout the world .\\n1\\t\"\n"
"        with open(file) as out:\n"
"            for doc in out:\n"
"                doc = json.loads(doc)\n"
"                doc_lines = {}\n"
"                for doc_line in doc['lines'].split('\\n'):\n"
"                    cols = doc_line.split('\\t')\n"
"                    if len(cols) > 1:\n"
"                        try:\n"
"                            doc_lines[int(cols[0])] = cols[1]\n"
"                        except Exception as e:\n"
"                            # print(cols)\n"
"                            pass\n"
"                wiki_docs[doc['id']] = doc_lines\n"
"\n"
"    for old_dir, new_dir in zip(args.dataset_dirs, args.output_paths):\n"
"        output_writer = open(new_dir, 'w')\n"
"        with open(old_dir) as out:\n"
"            for line in tqdm(out, desc=f'Processing dataset...{old_dir}', leave=True):\n"
"                line = json.loads(line)\n"
"                if line['label'] == 'NOT ENOUGH INFO':\n"
"                    continue\n"
"                for ann in line['evidence']:\n"
"                    sentences = []\n"
"                    for ann_sent in ann:  # [Annotation ID, Evidence ID, Wikipedia URL, sentence ID]\n"
"                        if ann_sent[2]:\n"
"                            wiki_url = unicodedata.normalize('NFC', ann_sent[2])\n"
"                            # [Wikipedia URL, sentence ID, sentence]\n"
"                            if wiki_url in wiki_docs:\n"
"                                sentence = wiki_docs[wiki_url].get(ann_sent[3], '')\n"
"                                if sentence != '':\n"
"                                    sentences.append([wiki_url, ann_sent[3], sentence])\n"
"                            else:\n"
"                                sentences = []\n"
"                                break\n"
"\n"
"                    if len(sentences) == 0:\n"
"                        continue\n"
"                    nli_line = line.copy()\n"
"                    nli_line['evidence'] = sentences\n"
"                    output_writer.write(json.dumps(nli_line) + '\\n')\n"
"        output_writer.close()\n"
"from xml.dom import minidom\n"
"from typing import AnyStr\n"
"from typing import List\n"
"from typing import Tuple\n"
"from typing import Set\n"
"import unicodedata\n"
"import json\n"
"import random\n"
"import string\n"
"\n"
"import torch\n"
"from torch.utils.data import Dataset\n"
"from transformers import PreTrainedTokenizer\n"
"from nltk.tokenize import word_tokenize\n"
"from nltk.corpus import stopwords\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"stopwords_en = set(stopwords.words('english'))\n"
"punc = set(string.punctuation)\n"
"with open('./data/indomain_words.txt') as f:\n"
"    eng_words = set([l.strip().lower() for l in f])\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def text_to_batch_transformer(claims: List, tokenizer: PreTrainedTokenizer, evidence: List) -> Tuple[List, List]:\n"
"    \"\"\"Turn a piece of text into a batch for transformer model\n"
"\n"
"    :param text: The text to tokenize and encode\n"
"    :param tokenizer: The tokenizer to use\n"
"    :param: text_pair: An optional second string (for multiple sentence sequences)\n"
"    :return: A list of IDs and a mask\n"
"    \"\"\"\n"
"    # Create the input string; first get a target word\n"
"    cands = [[w for w in word_tokenize(c) if w not in stopwords_en and w not in punc] for c in claims]\n"
"    #targets = [','.join([w.lower() for w in set(random.sample(cand, min(1,len(cand))) + random.sample(eng_words, 4))]) for cand in cands]\n"
"    # Using only candidates\n"
"    targets = [','.join([w.lower() for w in set(random.sample(cand, min(5,len(cand))))])for cand in cands]\n"
"    # # First get 5 possibel real candidates and 25 noise candidates\n"
"    # potential_words = [[w.lower() for w in set(random.sample(cand, min(5,len(cand))) + random.sample(eng_words, 25))] for cand in cands]\n"
"    # # Now randomly select 5 words from this list; we add more possible noise to give the model a better chance at generating good claims\n"
"    # # we want the model to just add words when they make sense, not force it to always pick 1-2 words\n"
"    # targets = [','.join(random.sample(pw, 5)) for pw in potential_words]\n"
"    texts = [f\"{target}||{evid}||{claim}\" for target,evid,claim in zip(targets, evidence, claims)]\n"
"    input_ids = [tokenizer.encode(t, max_length=tokenizer.max_len-1) + [tokenizer.eos_token_id] for t in texts]\n"
"\n"
"    masks = [[1] * len(i) for i in input_ids]\n"
"\n"
"    return input_ids, masks\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def collate_batch_transformer(input_data: Tuple) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n"
"    input_ids = [i[0][0] for i in input_data]\n"
"    masks = [i[1][0] for i in input_data]\n"
"\n"
"    max_length = max([len(i) for i in input_ids])\n"
"\n"
"    input_ids = [(i + [0] * (max_length - len(i))) for i in input_ids]\n"
"    masks = [(m + [0] * (max_length - len(m))) for m in masks]\n"
"\n"
"    assert (all(len(i) == max_length for i in input_ids))\n"
"    assert (all(len(m) == max_length for m in masks))\n"
"    return torch.tensor(input_ids), torch.tensor(masks)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def collate_batch_transformer_with_index(input_data: Tuple) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, List]:\n"
"    return collate_batch_transformer(input_data) + ([i[-1] for i in input_data],)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"class GPT2FeverDataset(Dataset):\n"
"    def __init__(self, data_dir: str, tokenizer: PreTrainedTokenizer):\n"
"        super().__init__()\n"
"        self._dataset = []\n"
"        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\n"
"        with open(data_dir) as out:\n"
"            for line in out:\n"
"                line = json.loads(line)\n"
"                self._dataset.append(line)\n"
"\n"
"    def filter_dataset(self, labels: Set[AnyStr]):\n"
"        \"\"\"\n"
"        Filters the dataset to only samples with the provided labels\n"
"        :param labels: A list of valid labels\n"
"        \"\"\"\n"
"        self._dataset = [ex for ex in self._dataset if ex['label'] in labels]\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"    def __len__(self):\n"
"        return len(self._dataset)\n"
"\n"
"    def __getitem__(self, item):\n"
"        \"\"\"\n"
"        :return:\n"
"        {'id': 163803,\n"
"        'verifiable': 'VERIFIABLE',\n"
"        'label': 'SUPPORTS',\n"
"        'claim': 'Ukrainian Soviet Socialist Republic was a founding participant of the UN.',\n"
"        'evidence': [['Ukrainian_Soviet_Socialist_Republic', 7,\n"
"            'The Ukrainian SSR was a founding member of the United Nations , although it was legally represented by the\n"
"            All-Union state in its affairs with countries outside of the Soviet Union .'\n"
"        ]]}\n"
"        \"\"\"\n"
"        row = self._dataset[item]\n"
"        claim = row['claim']\n"
"        evidence = ' '.join(r[2] for r in row['evidence'])\n"
"        input_ids, masks = text_to_batch_transformer([claim], self.tokenizer, [evidence])\n"
"        return input_ids, masks, item# E1: FEVER, Physics Questions and Bird evidence review\n"
"\n"

"import collections\n"
"import gzip\n"
"import hashlib\n"
"import json\n"
"import sys\n"
"import unicodedata\n"
"from pathlib import Path\n"
"\n"
"ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n"
"REPO = ROOT.parents[3]\n"
"INSTALLED = Path('/mnt/20t/xubuqiang/datasets/fever/valid.jsonl')\n"
"CHECK_ONLY = '--check' in sys.argv[1:]\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def emit(path, raw):\n"
"    if CHECK_ONLY:\n"
"        assert path.read_bytes() == raw, 'Reproduction differs: ' + str(path)\n"
"    else:\n"
"        path.write_bytes(raw)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def sha(data):\n"
"    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def canonical(value):\n"
"    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,\n"
"                      separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode()\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def normalized(page, line):\n"
"    return [unicodedata.normalize('NFC', page), int(line)]\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def group_pairs(group):\n"
"    return [normalized(e[2], e[3]) for e in group if e[2] is not None and e[3] is not None]\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def pair_set(pairs):\n"
"    return frozenset(map(tuple, pairs))\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def main():\n"
"    receipts = []\n"
"    for receipt_path in sorted(ROOT.glob('*.receipt.json')):\n"
"        receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text())\n"
"        packed = (ROOT / receipt['body_gzip']).read_bytes()\n"
"        raw = gzip.decompress(packed)\n"
"        assert sha(raw) == receipt['body_sha256']\n"
"        assert len(raw) == receipt['body_bytes']\n"
"        assert sha(packed) == receipt['gzip_sha256']\n"
"        receipts.append({'receipt': receipt_path.name, 'status': receipt['status'],\n"
"                         'body_bytes': len(raw), 'body_sha256': sha(raw),\n"
"                         'error': receipt['error']})\n"
"        if receipt['status'] == 200 and receipt_path.stem not in ['fever-dev.receipt', 'original-zenodo.receipt']:\n"
"            if receipt_path.stem in ['fever-download-page.receipt', 'producer-readme.receipt']:\n"
"                quoted = b''.join((json.dumps(line, ensure_ascii=False) + '\\n').encode()\n"
"                                  for line in raw.decode().splitlines(keepends=True))\n"

"        as_set = pair_set(pairs)\n"
"        exact_set_indices = [i for i, g in enumerate(groups) if as_set == pair_set(g)]\n"

"[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\\nWall time 4.7 seconds\\nOutput:\\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{\"chunk_id\":\"436df5\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.00000388,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":0,\"output\":\"\"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\\nFILE producer-readme\\n1 # Generating Label Cohesive and Well-Formed Adversarial Claims\\n2 This is the code base for the paper [Generating Label Cohesive and Well-Formed Adversarial Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2009.08205) to be presented at EMNLP 2020.\\n3 \\n4 <p align=\"center\">\\n5   <img src=\"architecture.png\" width=\"450\" alt=\"Adversarial Architecture\">\\n6 </p>\\n7 \\n8 Adversarial attacks reveal important vulnerabilities and flaws of trained models. \\n9 One potent type of attack are *universal adversarial triggers*, which are individual n-grams that, \\n10 when appended to instances of a class under attack, can trick a model into predicting a target class. \\n11 However, for inference tasks such as fact checking, these triggers often inadvertently *invert \\n12 the meaning of instances* they are inserted in. In addition, such attacks produce *semantically \\n13 nonsensical inputs*, as they simply concatenate triggers to existing samples. \\n14 Here, we investigate how to **generate adversarial attacks against fact checking systems that \\n15 preserve the ground truth meaning and are semantically valid**. We extend the HotFlip attack \\n16 algorithm used for universal trigger generation by jointly minimising the target class loss \\n17 of a fact checking model and the **entailment class loss of an auxiliary natural language inference \\n18 model**. We then train a **conditional language model to generate semantically valid statements**, \\n19 which include the found universal triggers. We find that the generated attacks maintain the \\n20 directionality and semantic validity of the claim better than previous work.\\n21 \\n22 \\n23 \\n24 ## Citation\\n25 ```bib\\n26 @article{atanasova2020generating,\\n27   title={Generating Label Cohesive and Well-Formed Adversarial Claims},\\n28   author={Atanasova, Pepa and Wright, Dustin and Augenstein, Isabelle},\\n29   booktitle={Proceedings of the 2020 conference on empirical methods in natural language processing (EMNLP)}\\n30   year={2020}\\n31 }\\n32 ```\\n33 \\n34 \\n35 # Dataset Preparation\\n36 The data used for training a fact checking model can be found at [HuggingFace fever_gold_evidence dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/copenlu/fever_gold_evidence)\\n37 \\n38 1. We join each claim with its gold evidence sentences from the Wikipedia pages. \\n39 This is done for SUPPORTS and REFUTES classes as NEI do not have evidence.<br>\\n40 `export PYTHONPATH=./`<br>\\n41 `python scripts/create_nli_dataset.py --dataset_dirs data/paper_train.jsonl --wiki_dir data/wiki-pages/wiki-pages/ --output_paths data/train_sr_nli.jsonl`\\n42 \\n43 Where paper_train.jsonl is an example path to a FEVER\\'s dataset file in jsonl format, and wiki-pages is an example path to the Wikipedia dump from the FEVER dataset.\\n44 \\n45 2. We then collect evidence sentences for the NEI class using the Papelo system from FEVER\\'2018 \\n46 and merge the outputs of the system for the NEI class with the gold evidence sentences collected in the previous step.<br>\\n47 `PYTHONPATH=fever2018-retrieval/src`<br>\\n48 `python fever2018-retrieval/src/scripts/retrieval/ir.py --db data/fever/fever.db --model data/index/fever-tfidf-ngram\\\\=2-hash\\\\=16777216-tokenizer\\\\=simple.npz --in-file data/fever-data/train_nei.jsonl --out-file data/fever/train_nei.sentences.p5.s5.jsonl --max-page 2 --max-sent 2`\\n49 <br><br>\\n50 `python scripts/aggregate_papelo_nei.py --dataset_dirs data/fever/train_nei.sentences.p5.s5.jsonl --wiki_dir data/wiki-pages/wiki-pages/ --output_paths data/train_nei_nli.jsonl`\\n51 <br><br>\\n52 `python scripts/merge_nli_files.py --sr_file data/train_sr_nli.jsonl --nei_file data/train_nei_nli.jsonl --output_path data/train_nli.jsonl`\\n53 \\n54 # Builder Models\\n55 We train a RoBERTa model on the data prepared in the previous step which is used for the adversarial attacks <br>\\n56 `python builders/train_transformer.py --gpu --lr 2e-5 --model_path fever_roberta_2e5 --batch_size 8 --type roberta`\\n57 \\n58 # Trigger Generation\\n59 Triggers can be generated with varying the weights for the FC and the NLI objective. For example, for attacks only using the FC objective, the weights are --fc_w 1 --nli_w 0 : <br>\\n60 `python attack_multiple_objectives/attack_fc_nli_trans.py --gpu --model_path fever_roberta_2e5 --fc_model_type roberta --batch_size 10 --trigger_length 3 --labels 3 --epochs 20 --fc_w 1 --nli_w 0 --attack_class SUPPORTS --target REFUTES`\\n61 \\n62 The resulting triggers are serialized to the attack_results directory.\\n63 The code is based on the code of the [Universal Adversarial Attacks](https://github.com/Eric-Wallace/universal-triggers)\\n64 ## To evaluate the generated triggers:\\n65 `python attack_multiple_objectives/eval_triggers.py --gpu --model_path fever_roberta_2e5 --fc_model_type roberta --batch_size 4 --labels 3 --triggers_file data/fc10_nli0_ppl0_SUPPORTS_to_REFUTES_1_triggers.tsv --attack_class SUPPORTS`\\n66 \\n67 # Conditional Adversarial Claim Generation\\n68 \\n69 To generate adversarial claims, first train a GPT-2 model for the particular types of claims you wish to generate (SUPPORTS, REFUTES, or NOT ENOUGH INFO). The following is an example of how to create a model which generates SUPPORTS claims.\\n70 ```bash\\n71 python train_gpt2_model.py \\\\\\n72   --dataset_loc data/train_nli.jsonl \\\\\\n73   --val_dataset data/dev_nli.jsonl \\\\\\n74   --train_pct 0.9 \\\\\\n75   --n_gpu 1 \\\\\\n76   --n_epochs 20 \\\\\\n77   --seed 1000 \\\\\\n78   --model_dir gpt2_models/1_candidate_4_random_supports \\\\\\n79   --batch_size 4 \\\\\\n80   --lr 0.00003 \\\\\\n81   --target_class \"SUPPORTS\" \\\\\\n82   --run_name 1c_4e_supports \\\\\\n83   --tags \"gpt2 training supports\"\\n84 ```\\n85 \\n86 You can then generate adversarial examples via the following. You will need to have a trained BERT model on SNLI as the `nli_model` parameter.\\n87 \\n88 ```bash\\n89 python generate_gpt2_sentences.py \\\\\\n90   --dataset_loc data/test_nli.jsonl  \\\\\\n91   --model_loc gpt2_models/1_candidate_4_random_supports/model.pth \\\\\\n92   --triggers_file data/fc10_nli0_ppl0_SUPPORTS_to_REFUTES_1_triggers.tsv \\\\\\n93   --output_file gpt2_models/1_candidate_4_random_supports/generation_s_to_r.txt \\\\\\n94   --target_class SUPPORTS \\\\\\n95   --nli_model transformer_snli_2e5_1\\n96 ```\\n97 \\n98 # Final generated claims\\n99 The final generated claims for both experiments can be found in `final_labelled_annotations.tsv`. The columns are as follows:\\n100  - original file: Internal filename which generated this claim. Files containing \\'no_stsb\\' are for claims generated with triggers from the model trained without the stsb objective.\\n101  - idx: An internal index for the claim.\\n102  - claim: The generated claim.\\n103  - evidence: The evidence paired with the claim.\\n104  - triggers: The triggers used to generate the claim.\\n105  - semantically coherent: Claim quality score.\\n106  - label: The final adjudicated label of the claim,evidence pair.\\n107  - title: The title of the wikipedia article originating the evidence.\\n108  - ann1_label: The original label from the first annotator.\\n109  - ann1_label: The original label from the second annotator.\\n110  - pred_label: The predicted label of the external fact checking model.\\n111  - control_group: Whether or not the STSB objective was used to generate the triggers (0: no stsb; 1: uses stsb) \\n\\nFILE producer-merge\\n1 \"\"\"Add \\'NOT ENOUGH INFO\\' instances generated with the Papelo system.\"\"\"\\n2 import argparse\\n3 import "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n"
"\"\"\"Validate the source-only blocker index; never import adapters or open receipts.\"\"\"\n"
"from __future__ import annotations\n"
"\n"
"import argparse\n"
"import ast\n"
"import hashlib\n"
"import json\n"
"from pathlib import Path\n"
"\n"
"ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\n"
"REGISTRY = ROOT / \"registry/dataset-blockers.json\"\n"
"HISTORY = ROOT / \"tests/fixtures/dataset_blockers/history-codex178.json\"\n"
"# Pin the review baseline independently of the editable registry.\n"
"HISTORY_SHA256 = \"4c778d435f4147eb04dbcee4e37a2bac3f0ac5a55584e3adf252ab12972d1300\"\n"
"STATUSES = {\"code-solvable\", \"external\", \"runtime\", \"measurement\"}\n"
"FAMILIES = {\n"
"    \"selected-only-privacy\", \"source-provenance\", \"scorer-dependency-network\",\n"
"    \"trusted-verifier-runtime\", \"artifact-only-export\", \"filesystem-quota\",\n"
"    \"official-scorer-execution\", \"snapshot-cleanup\", \"measurement-coverage\",\n"
"    \"metric-comparability\", \"platform-integrity\", \"actor-protocol\",\n"
"    \"termination-audit\", \"contract-refusal\", \"journal-coverage\", \"documentation-conflict\",\n"
"}\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"class RegistryError(ValueError):\n"
"    pass\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _require(condition, message: str) -> None:\n"
"    if not condition:\n"
"        raise RegistryError(message)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _unique_object(pairs):\n"
"    obj = {}\n"
"    for key, value in pairs:\n"
"        _require(key not in obj, f\"duplicate JSON key: {key}\")\n"
"        obj[key] = value\n"
"    return obj\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _reject_constant(value):\n"
"    raise RegistryError(f\"nonfinite JSON constant: {value}\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def read_json(path: Path):\n"
"    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"),\n"
"                      object_pairs_hook=_unique_object, parse_constant=_reject_constant)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def literal_declarations(path: Path, names: set[str]) -> dict:\n"
"    \"\"\"Read only module-level literals, without running even taxonomy imports.\"\"\"\n"
"    found = {}\n"
"    for node in ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), filename=str(path)).body:\n"
"        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):\n"
"            targets = node.targets\n"
"        elif isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign):\n"
"            targets = [node.target]\n"
"        else:\n"
"            continue\n"
"        for target in targets:\n"
"            if isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id in names:\n"
"                _require(target.id not in found, f\"{path.name}: repeated {target.id}\")\n"
"                try:\n"
"                    found[target.id] = ast.literal_eval(node.value)\n"
"                except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:\n"
"                    raise RegistryError(f\"{path.name}: {target.id} must be a literal\") from exc\n"
"    return found\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def published_declarations(root: Path = ROOT) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n"
"    \"\"\"Conservative chooser coverage, including unprovisioned/refused datasets.\n"
"\n"
"    `data.scaffolds.registry.published()` imports adapters and may omit an import failure.\n"
"    Reading KIND declarations covers every publishable source without loading a\n"
"    mixed task pool. A missing literal KIND retains the registry's fixture default.\n"
"    \"\"\"\n"
"    return tuple(path.stem for path in sorted((root / \"data\" / \"datasets\").glob(\"*.py\"))\n"
"                 if literal_declarations(path, {\"KIND\"}).get(\"KIND\") == \"dataset\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _fields(obj, expected: set[str], label: str) -> None:\n"
"    _require(isinstance(obj, dict), f\"{label}: expected object\")\n"
"    _require(set(obj) == expected, f\"{label}: fields must be {sorted(expected)}\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _text(value, label: str) -> None:\n"
"    _require(isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip()), f\"{label}: empty text\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _unique_strings(values, label: str) -> None:\n"
"    _require(isinstance(values, list), f\"{label}: expected list\")\n"
"    for value in values:\n"
"        _text(value, label)\n"
"    _require(len(set(values)) == len(values), f\"{label}: duplicates\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _evidence(items, root: Path, label: str) -> None:\n"
"    _require(isinstance(items, list) and bool(items), f\"{label}: evidence required\")\n"
"    kinds = []\n"
"    for item in items:\n"
"        _fields(item, {\"kind\", \"path\", \"locator\"}, label)\n"
"        kind, path = item[\"kind\"], item[\"path\"]\n"
"        _text(path, label)\n"
"        _text(item[\"locator\"], label)\n"
"        _require(kind in {\"repository\", \"external-reference\"}, f\"{label}: evidence kind\")\n"
"        kinds.append(kind)\n"
"        if kind == \"external-reference\":\n"
"            # This is a historical citation, not a file we have verified or may open.\n"
"            _require(Path(path).is_absolute(), f\"{label}: external reference must be absolute\")\n"
"            continue\n"
"        rel = Path(path)\n"
"        _require(not rel.is_absolute() and \"..\" not in rel.parts,\n"
"                 f\"{label}: repository evidence must stay inside root\")\n"
"        source = root / rel\n"
"        _require(source.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()), f\"{label}: evidence escapes root\")\n"
"        _require(source.is_file(), f\"{label}: missing evidence {path}\")\n"
"        _require(item[\"locator\"] in source.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"),\n"
"                 f\"{label}: stale evidence locator in {path}\")\n"
"    _require(\"repository\" in kinds, f\"{label}: repository observation required\")\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def _blocker(blocker, root: Path, label: str) -> None:\n"
"    _fields(blocker, {\"id\", \"family\", \"status\", \"observed\", \"evidence\", \"next_action\"}, label)\n"
"    for field in (\"id\", \"observed\", \"next_action\"):\n"
"        _text(blocker[field], f\"{label}.{field}\")\n"
"    _require(blocker[\"family\"] in FAMILIES, f\"{label}: unknown blocker family\")\n"
"    _require(blocker[\"status\"] in STATUSES, f\"{label}: unknown status\")\n"
"    _evidence(blocker[\"evidence\"], root, label)\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def validate(document: dict, *, root: Path = ROOT) -> dict:\n"
"    _fields(document, {\"schema_version\", \"review\", \"shared_blockers\", \"datasets\"}, \"registry\")\n"
"    _require(type(document[\"schema_version\"]) is int and document[\"schema_version\"] == 1,\n"
"             \"unsupported schema version\")\n"
"    _fields(document[\"review\"], {\"date\", \"scope\", \"production_authorized\", \"notes\"}, \"review\")\n"
"    _text(document[\"review\"][\"date\"], \"review.date\")\n"
"    _require(document[\"review\"][\"scope\"] == \"repository-source-and-journal-only\", \"review scope\")\n"
"    _require(document[\"review\"][\"production_authorized\"] is False, \"registry cannot authorize production\")\n"
"    _unique_strings(document[\"review\"][\"notes\"], \"review.notes\")\n"
"\n"
"    taxonomy = literal_declarations(root / \"tools/dataset_classes.py\", {\"CAPABILITY\", \"EXERCISES\"})\n"
"    published = set(published_declarations(root))\n"
"    _require(set(taxonomy[\"CAPABILITY\"]) == published, \"taxonomy/published declarations differ\")\n"
"    _require(set(taxonomy[\"EXERCISES\"]) == published, \"exercise/published declarations differ\")\n"
"    _require(isinstance(document[\"shared_blockers\"], list), \"shared_blockers: expected list\")\n"
"    shared = set()\n"
"    for blocker in document[\"shared_blockers\"]:\n"
"        _blocker(blocker, root, \"shared blocker\")\n"
"        _require(blocker[\"id\"] not in shared, \"duplicate shared blocker\")\n"
"        shared.add(blocker[\"id\"])\n"
"\n"
"    _require(isinstance(document[\"datasets\"], list), \"datasets: expected list\")\n"
"    seen, history, run_ids = set(), {}, set()\n"
"    for row in document[\"datasets\"]:\n"
"        _fields(row, {\"dataset\", \"demand\", \"surface\", \"exercises\", \"shared_blockers\",\n"
"                      \"blockers\", \"historical_runs\"}, \"dataset\")\n"
"        name = row[\"dataset\"]\n"
"        _text(name, \"dataset name\")\n"
"        _require(name not in seen, f\"duplicate dataset: {name}\")\n"
"        _require(name in published, f\"unpublished dataset: {name}\")\n"
"        seen.add(name)\n"
"        _require((row[\"demand\"], row[\"surface\"]) == taxonomy[\"CAPABILITY\"][name],\n"
"                 f\"{name}: demand x surface drift\")\n"
"        _unique_strings(row[\"exercises\"], f\"{name}.exercises\")\n"
"        _require(row[\"exercises\"] == list(taxonomy[\"EXERCISES\"][name]), f\"{name}: exercise drift\")\n"
"        _unique_strings(row[\"shared_blockers\"], f\"{name}.shared_blockers\")\n"
"        _require(set(row[\"shared_blockers\"]) <= shared, f\"{name}: unknown shared blocker\")\n"
"        # An empty `blockers` list is allowed, and is a statement rather than a blank: it\n"
"        # says every requirement this dataset carried was reviewed and either met or\n"
"        # withdrawn by decision. `_fields` above already requires the key to be present, so\n"
"        # an accidentally-omitted list is still caught; what the old `bool(...)` added was\n"
"        # only the inability to express \"cleared\".\n"
"        #\n"
"        # This is deliberately NOT the same move as adding a value to `STATUSES` so a\n"
"        # finished blocker can stay parked in the registry with a status meaning \"done\".\n"
"        # That keeps a closed requirement counted as live and widens a closed vocabulary;\n"
"        # this removes a non-emptiness check so closure has somewhere to land. A dataset\n"
"        # cleared this way must have its decision recorded in a checked-in document, and\n"
"        # `scienceworld` is the case: see `docs/enforcement-proof-decision.md`.\n"
"        _require(isinstance(row[\"blockers\"], list),\n"
"                 f\"{name}: blockers must be a list\")\n"
"        ids = set()\n"
"        for blocker in row[\"blockers\"]:\n"
"            _blocker(blocker, root, name)\n"
"            _require(blocker[\"id\"] not in ids, f\"{name}: duplicate blocker\")\n"
"            ids.add(blocker[\"id\"])\n"
"        _require(isinstance(row[\"historical_runs\"], list), f\"{name}: history must be a list\")\n"
"        for run in row[\"historical_runs\"]:\n"
"            _fields(run, {\"record_id\", \"run_id\", \"status\", \"effect\", \"observation\", \"evidence\"}, name)\n"
"            _text(run[\"record_id\"], f\"{name}: historical record ID\")\n"
"            if run[\"run_id\"] is not None:\n"
"                _text(run[\"run_id\"], f\"{name}: run ID\")\n"
"                _require(run[\"run_id\"] not in run_ids, f\"{name}: duplicate historical run ID\")\n"
"                run_ids.add(run[\"run_id\"])\n"
"            _require(run[\"status\"] in {\"incomplete\", \"partial-measurement\", \"pending-review\"},\n"
"                     f\"{name}: historical incomplete cannot become complete\")\n"
"            _require(run[\"effect\"] is None, f\"{name}: historical incomplete cannot acquire an effect\")\n"
"            _text(run[\"observation\"], f\"{name}: historical observation\")\n"
"            _evidence(run[\"evidence\"], root, f\"{name}: historical evidence\")\n"
"            key = run[\"record_id\"]\n"
"            _require(key not in history, f\"duplicate historical record: {key}\")\n"
"            history[key] = {\"dataset\": name, \"record\": run}\n"
"    _require(seen == published, f\"missing published datasets: {sorted(published - seen)}\")\n"
"\n"
"    raw = HISTORY.read_bytes()\n"
"    _require(hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() == HISTORY_SHA256, \"historical baseline pin changed\")\n"
"    baseline = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=_unique_object, parse_constant=_reject_constant)\n"
"    for old in baseline[\"records\"]:\n"
"        key = old[\"record\"][\"record_id\"]\n"
"        _require(history.get(key) == old, f\"historical record removed or changed: {key}\")\n"
"    return {\"datasets\": len(seen), \"shared_blockers\": len(shared), \"historical_records\": len(history)}\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"def main(argv=None) -> int:\n"
"    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n"
"    parser.add_argument(\"--registry\", type=Path, default=REGISTRY)\n"
"    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n"
"    try:\n"
"        counts = validate(read_json(args.registry))\n"
"    except (RegistryError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:\n"
"        print(json.dumps({\"valid\": False, \"error\": str(exc)}))\n"
"        return 2\n"
"    print(json.dumps({\"valid\": True, **counts, \"production_authorized\": False}))\n"
"    return 0\n"
"\n"
"\n"
"if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n"
"    raise SystemExit(main())\n"

Flat conjunction versus singleton alternatives — source analysis only\n\n### FOUND — exact predicates and representation boundary\n\nThe producer emits one row for each original group, storing its available sentence triples in a flat list (@C:56, @C:57, @C:64, @C:72, @C:73, @C:74). Its conjunction meaning is the original group. The current local converter explicitly makes `groups = [[item] for item in groups]` when the flat-list branch matches (`data/scaffolds/qa/evidence_scorers.py:158`–`:164`, at base `eada00fc072d7006fb08cf7f4a750dbaa1437174`). It converts triple line numbers with `int()` and compares page/line membership in the first five predictions (`data/scaffolds/qa/evidence_scorers.py:156`–`:166`). This source conversion is the singleton behavior being compared; it is not the producer's documented conjunction conversion.\n\nThe pinned upstream scorer requires lists of `(string, int)` predictions (@S:3, @S:14, @S:18), compares uppercase labels (@S:22), extracts `[e[2], e[3]]` from each **nested group of four-field records** (@S:37, @S:39), requires every sentence of one group inside the prediction prefix (@S:41), and exempts correctly labelled NEI (@S:45). `fever_score` defaults to five pairs (@S:106) and passes that bound into strictness (@S:133). Calling `is_strictly_correct` alone with its default `None` does not impose five (@S:26, @S:33). This analysis uses the headline wrapper's five-pair default, not an invented helper default.\n\nFlat triples cannot be passed directly to that upstream implementation as though it understood their shape: it indexes annotation-style nested records (@S:39). For a valid source comparison, translate each pair `(page, int(line))` into `[null, null, page, int(line)]`; either place all records in **one group** (conjunction) or put each record in its **own group** (singletons). No translation or scorer was executed here. Pair normalization for the structural inventory is NFC-page / integer-line as in w-jc2 (@O:31); raw arbitrary page normalization equivalence is not claimed.\n\nLet `G` be the distinct normalized pairs of one exported non-NEI row, and `P5` the first five predicted pairs, with correct label and well-formed supplied predictions. Substituting the two groupings into the pinned source gives:\n\n```text\nconjunction strict pass  <=> G is a subset of P5\nsingleton strict pass    <=> G intersects P5\nstrict disagreement      <=> 0 < |G intersect P5| < |G|\n```\n\nThe equivalence follows directly from the outer group loop and inner `all` (@S:37, @S:41). Therefore singleton conversion cannot decrease this row's strict predicate relative to its **same exported group**; it can increase it when the label is correct and evidence is a nonempty proper subset. Wrong labels fail both (@S:30, @S:50); correctly labelled NEI passes both (@S:45). Pairs after position five cannot repair an incomplete prefix (@S:41, @S:106). If `|G| > 5`, the conjunction cannot pass at this bound, whereas a singleton can pass with one hit. Duplicate predictions consume positions; they cannot supply more than five distinct pairs. The installed non-NEI rows contain no duplicate gold pairs (exact parsing output below).\n\nFor the other returned metrics, label accuracy is unchanged (@S:130, @S:143). Evidence precision uses the union of pairs, so changing only grouping leaves it unchanged (@S:53, @S:58, @S:65). Complete-group recall changes under the same nonempty-proper-subset condition even if the label is wrong (@S:69, @S:71, @S:80, @S:83). F1 can consequently change when the aggregate formula is defined (@S:145, @S:146, @S:148); the source has an unguarded division when `pr + rec == 0`, so an undefined/error result is not a zero. The conclusion concerns strict correctness and source-defined metrics, not a new observed aggregate score. Group-per-row conversion also changes original-claim weighting/denominator: an original claim with several exported groups becomes several rows (@C:56, @C:74; prior 15,935 rows versus 9,835 distinct original IDs @O:25). Grouping equivalence alone would not establish original-dev aggregate comparability.\n\n### FOUND — precisely which installed rows are exposed\n\nThe exact command `python3 /mnt/20t/xubuqiang/hg-work/fevconv/parse-installed.py` only parsed installed bytes, recomputed their known SHA-256, and counted pair-set sizes; it did not import or call a scorer. Full exact output and reproduction program are in validation. An exact row identifier rule is: `label != \"NOT ENOUGH INFO\" and len({(NFC(e[0]), int(e[1])) for e in row[\"evidence\"]}) > 1`. This strengthens the witness beyond mere exported-group comparison. The remaining five multisentence rows still differ from their own conjunction, but every one of their singleton pairs satisfies some original complete group under the established normalized mapping (difference between the prior 1,400 / 1,395 predicates @O:20, @O:21; audit definition @A:108). That does not mean the full original-gold and exported-singleton predicates are equivalent for arbitrary predictions: the original can have alternative groups not present in this row. "   148\t    pr = (macro_precision / macro_precision_hits) if macro_precision_hits > 0 else 1.0\n"
"   149\t    rec = (macro_recall / macro_recall_hits) if macro_recall_hits > 0 else 0.0\n"
"   150\t\n"
"   151\t    f1 = 2.0 * pr * rec / (pr + rec)\n"
"   152\t\n"
"?? registry/w-fevconv-receipts.md\n"
